- **compiler-abi**: ABI and calling convention expert — covers System V AMD64 ABI, Windows x64 calling convention, struct layout and alignment, register vs stack parameter passing, red zone, and WebAssembly ABI. Use when generating native code that interoperates with C, implementing FFI, or designing calling conventions for a new language. ([onehr/compiler-expert](https://github.com/onehr/compiler-expert/tree/HEAD/skills/compiler-abi) · ★1)
- **compiler-ac85**: Agent compiler for Intel 8085 (ac85): emit Zilog assembly for z88dk-z80asm -m8085 from standard C90. Data model, SMALLC ABI, stack frames, HL as ALU bus, BC/DE parking, 8085 extended ops, C90 shape → ISA lowering. Use for /compiler-ac85, "agent compiler 8085", "C compiler guide for 8085", "compile this C to 8085 assembly", "8085 C lowering". - **complaint-escalation**: 1. **事件定级**：根据影响金额、客户身份、传播风险、监管关注度进行L1-L4分级 2. **合规审查**：核实是否涉及《消保法》规定的退一赔三、《食品安全法》退一赔十等法定赔偿，确认监管时效要求 3. **处理策略制定**： - 客户诉求合理性评估 - 底线方案与最优方案设计 - 补偿权限与审批流程确认 - 话术框架与沟通节奏规划 4. **跨部门协同**：明确需要法务、产品、技术、公关等部门配合的事项和时间节点 5. **闭环与复盘**：制定跟进计划、满意度回访方案、根因修复追踪 ([aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform](https://github.com/aws-samples/sample-agentcore-demo-platform/tree/HEAD/super-agent/industry-packs/industry-pack-customer-service/digital-twins/%E5%AE%A2%E6%9C%8D%E6%80%BB%E7%9B%91/skills/complaint-escalation) · ★27 · 2 copies)

- **complete-stories**: Marks Scrum stories Done and rolls epic/backlog status up once every child is complete. BLOCKS completion if any story AC checkbox is unchecked, any dependency is not Done, any referenced deliverable is missing, or any epic-level AC is still open. Uses Edit (not Write) to flip only the Status cell and AC checkboxes, preserving every other line. If the gate blocks on ANY target, NOTHING is edited — the run is atomic from the user's view. Use when the user asks to: - Mark STORY-NN-NNN complete / close a story - Close out EPIC-NN / roll up epic status - "Are we done with sprint 3 yet?"
